L0040: function rankingToPredictions(ranking = [], opts = {}) {
L0041:   const limit = Math.max(1, Number(opts.limit || 10));
L0042: 
L0043:   const rows = (Array.isArray(ranking) ? ranking : [])
L0044:     .map((item) => {
L0045:       const grupo = pad2(item.grupo || item.grupoNum || item.group || item.id);
L0046:       const aparicoes = toNum(item.total ?? item.apar ?? item.count ?? 0);
L0047: 
L0048:       return {
L0049:         grupo,
L0050:         animal: item.animal || item.nome || ANIMALS[grupo] || null,
L0051:         aparicoes,
L0052:         raw: item,
L0053:       };
L0054:     })
L0055:     .filter((x) => /^\d{2}$/.test(x.grupo))
L0056:     .sort((a, b) => b.aparicoes - a.aparicoes || Number(a.grupo) - Number(b.grupo))
L0057:     .slice(0, limit);
L0058: 
L0059:   const max = rows.reduce((m, x) => Math.max(m, x.aparicoes), 0) || 1;
L0060: 
L0061:   return rows.map((x, idx) => {
L0062:     const score = Math.round((x.aparicoes / max) * 100);
L0063:     const confidence = Math.max(1, Math.round(score - idx * 2));
L0064: 
L0065:     return {
L0066:       type: "grupo",
L0067:       grupo: x.grupo,
L0068:       animal: x.animal,
L0069:       score,
L0070:       confidence,
L0071:       reasons: [
L0072:         `Ranking V1: ${x.aparicoes} aparições no recorte analisado`,
L0073:         `Posição ${idx + 1} entre os grupos avaliados`,
L0074:       ],
L0075:       signals: {
L0076:         aparicoes: x.aparicoes,
L0077:         rankPosition: idx + 1,
L0078:         engine: "score_engine_v1",
L0079:       },
L0080:     };
L0081:   });

L0006: /**

L0008:  *
L0009:  * Consome evidências.
L0010:  * Não aplica regra cega isolada.
L0011:  */
L0012: 
L0013: function normalizeEvidenceValue(value) {
L0014:   const n = Number(value);
L0015:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n <= 0) return 0;
L0016:   return n;
L0017: }
L0018: 
L0019: function calculateEvidenceStrength(evidenceList = []) {
L0020:   const list = Array.isArray(evidenceList) ? evidenceList : [];
L0021: 
L0022:   if (!list.length) {
L0023:     return {
L0024:       score: 0,
L0025:       confidence: 0,
L0026:       reasons: ["Sem evidências suficientes."],
L0027:       signals: {},
L0028:     };
L0029:   }
L0030: 
L0031:   const values = list.map((e) => normalizeEvidenceValue(e.value));
L0032:   const total = values.reduce((acc, v) => acc + v, 0);
L0033:   const max = Math.max(...values, 1);
L0034: 
L0035:   const score = Math.min(100, Math.round((total / max) * 50));
L0036:   const confidence = Math.min(100, Math.round(score * Math.min(1, list.length / 3)));
L0037: 
L0038:   const reasons = list.flatMap((e) => Array.isArray(e.reasons) ? e.reasons : []);
L0039: 
L0040:   const signals = {};
L0041:   for (const e of list) {
L0042:     signals[e.module] = e.evidence || {};
L0043:   }
L0044: 
L0045:   return {
L0046:     score,
L0047:     confidence,
L0048:     reasons,
L0049:     signals,
L0050:   };
L0051: }
L0052: 
L0053: function scoreItem(item = {}, context = {}) {
L0054:   const collected = collectEvidence({
L0055:     item,
L0056:     context,
L0057:     config: scoreConfig,
L0058:   });
L0059: 
L0060:   const strength = calculateEvidenceStrength(collected.evidence);
L0061: 
L0062:   return {
L0063:     ...item,
L0064:     score: strength.score,
L0065:     confidence: strength.confidence,
L0066:     reasons: strength.reasons,
L0067:     signals: strength.signals,
L0068:     evidenceCount: collected.count,
L0069:     evidenceModules: collected.modules,
L0070:   };
L0071: }
L0072: 
L0073: function scoreRanking(items = [], context = {}) {
L0074: 
L0075:   return (Array.isArray(items) ? items : [])
L0076:     .map(item => scoreItem(item, context))
L0077:     .sort((a, b) => {
L0078: 
L0079:       if (b.score !== a.score)
L0080:         return b.score - a.score;
L0081: 
L0082:       if (b.confidence !== a.confidence)
L0083:         return b.confidence - a.confidence;
L0084: 
L0085:       return (Number(b.scoreProb || 0) - Number(a.scoreProb || 0));
L0086: 
L0087:     });
L0088: 
L0089: }
L0090: 
L0091: module.exports = {
L0092:   scoreItem,
L0093:   scoreRanking,
L0094:   calculateEvidenceStrength,
L0095: };

L0030: function isISODateStrict(s) {
L0031:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
L0032:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
L0033: 
L0034:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
L0035:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
L0036: 
L0037:   return (
L0038:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
L0039:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
L0040:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
L0041:   );
L0042: }

L0028: function isISODateStrict(s) {
L0029:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
L0030:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
L0031:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
L0032:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
L0033:   return dt.getUTCFullYear() === y && dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 && dt.getUTCDate() === d;
L0034: }

L0045: function isISODate(s) {
L0046:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
L0047:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
L0048: 
L0049:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
L0050:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
L0051: 
L0052:   return (
L0053:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
L0054:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
L0055:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
L0056:   );
L0057: }

L0007: function isYmdStrict(s) {
L0008:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
L0009:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
L0010: 
L0011:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map((n) => Number(n));
L0012:   if (!Number.isFinite(y) || !Number.isFinite(m) || !Number.isFinite(d)) return false;
L0013:   if (m < 1 || m > 12) return false;
L0014:   if (d < 1 || d > 31) return false;
L0015: 
L0016:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
L0017:   return (
L0018:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
L0019:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
L0020:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
L0021:   );
L0022: }

L0038: function parseArgs(argv) {
L0039:   const args = {
L0040:     reportPathArg: null,
L0041:     gapsPathArg: null,
L0042:   };
L0043: 
L0044:   const a = argv.slice(2).map(String);
L0045: 
L0046:   // help
L0047:   if (a.includes("--help") || a.includes("-h")) usage(0);
L0048: 
L0049:   // suporte flags longas simples
L0050:   for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
L0051:     const cur = a[i];
L0052: 
L0053:     if (cur === "--report") {
L0054:       args.reportPathArg = a[i + 1];
L0055:       i++;
L0056:       continue;
L0057:     }
L0058: 
L0059:     if (cur.startsWith("--report=")) {
L0060:       args.reportPathArg = cur.split("=").slice(1).join("=");
L0061:       continue;
L0062:     }
L0063: 
L0064:     if (cur === "--gaps") {
L0065:       args.gapsPathArg = a[i + 1];
L0066:       i++;
L0067:       continue;
L0068:     }
L0069: 
L0070:     if (cur.startsWith("--gaps=")) {
L0071:       args.gapsPathArg = cur.split("=").slice(1).join("=");
L0072:       continue;
L0073:     }
L0074:   }
L0075: 
L0076:   // fallback posicional: <report> [gaps]
L0077:   const positionals = a.filter((x) => !x.startsWith("-"));
L0078:   if (!args.reportPathArg) args.reportPathArg = positionals[0] || null;
L0079:   if (!args.gapsPathArg) args.gapsPathArg = positionals[1] || null;
L0080: 
L0081:   return args;
L0082: }

L0015: function isISODateStrict(s) {

L0024:     dt.getUTCDate() === d

L0264: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
L0265:   const s0 = String(value ?? "").trim();
L0266:   if (!s0) return "";
L0267: 
L0268:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
L0269: 
L0270:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
L0271:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
L0272: 
L0273:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
L0274:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
L0275: 
L0276:   const mHm = s.match(/^(\d{3,4})$/);
L0277:   if (mHm) {
L0278:     const hh = String(mHm[1]).slice(0, -2);
L0279:     const mm = String(mHm[1]).slice(-2);
L0280:     if (/^\d{1,2}$/.test(hh) && /^\d{2}$/.test(mm)) {
L0281:       return `${pad2(hh)}:${mm}`;
L0282:     }
L0283:   }
L0284: 
L0285:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
L0286:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
L0287: 
L0288:   return s0.trim();
L0289: }

L0027: function drawTs(draw) {
L0028:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
L0029:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
L0030:   if (!y || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
L0031: 
L0032:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map(Number);
L0033:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
L0034:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
L0035:   const add = mins >= 0 ? mins * 60 * 1000 : 0;
L0036:   return base + add;
L0037: }

L0027: function drawTs(draw) {
L0028:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
L0029:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
L0030:   if (!y || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
L0031: 
L0032:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map(Number);
L0033:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
L0034:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
L0035:   const add = mins >= 0 ? mins * 60 * 1000 : 0;
L0036: 
L0037:   return base + add;
L0038: }
L0039:

L0041:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);

L0043:   if (!y || !h) return "";
L0044:   return `${y}|${h}`;

L0001: "use strict";
L0002: 
L0003: import scoreConfig from "./scoreConfig";
L0004: import { collectEvidence } from "./evidenceEngine";
L0005: 
L0006: /**
L0007:  * Score Engine V2 inicial
L0008:  *
L0009:  * Consome evidências.
L0010:  * Não aplica regra cega isolada.
L0011:  */
L0012: 
L0013: function normalizeEvidenceValue(value) {
L0014:   const n = Number(value);
L0015:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n <= 0) return 0;
L0016:   return n;
L0017: }
L0018: 
L0019: function calculateEvidenceStrength(evidenceList = []) {
L0020:   const list = Array.isArray(evidenceList) ? evidenceList : [];
L0021: 
L0022:   if (!list.length) {
L0023:     return {
L0024:       score: 0,
L0025:       confidence: 0,
L0026:       reasons: ["Sem evidências suficientes."],
L0027:       signals: {},
L0028:     };
L0029:   }
L0030: 
L0031:   const values = list.map((e) => normalizeEvidenceValue(e.value));
L0032:   const total = values.reduce((acc, v) => acc + v, 0);
L0033:   const max = Math.max(...values, 1);
L0034: 
L0035:   const score = Math.min(100, Math.round((total / max) * 50));
L0036:   const confidence = Math.min(100, Math.round(score * Math.min(1, list.length / 3)));
L0037: 
L0038:   const reasons = list.flatMap((e) => Array.isArray(e.reasons) ? e.reasons : []);
L0039: 
L0040:   const signals = {};
L0041:   for (const e of list) {
L0042:     signals[e.module] = e.evidence || {};
L0043:   }
L0044: 
L0045:   return {
L0046:     score,
L0047:     confidence,
L0048:     reasons,
L0049:     signals,
L0050:   };
L0051: }
L0052: 
L0053: function scoreItem(item = {}, context = {}) {
L0054:   const collected = collectEvidence({
L0055:     item,
L0056:     context,
L0057:     config: scoreConfig,
L0058:   });
L0059: 
L0060:   const strength = calculateEvidenceStrength(collected.evidence);
L0061: 
L0062:   return {
L0063:     ...item,
L0064:     score: strength.score,
L0065:     confidence: strength.confidence,
L0066:     reasons: strength.reasons,
L0067:     signals: strength.signals,
L0068:     evidenceCount: collected.count,
L0069:     evidenceModules: collected.modules,
L0070:   };
L0071: }
L0072: 
L0073: function scoreRanking(items = [], context = {}) {
L0074: 
L0075:   return (Array.isArray(items) ? items : [])
L0076:     .map(item => scoreItem(item, context))
L0077:     .sort((a, b) => {
L0078: 
L0079:       if (b.score !== a.score)
L0080:         return b.score - a.score;
L0081: 
L0082:       if (b.confidence !== a.confidence)
L0083:         return b.confidence - a.confidence;
L0084: 
L0085:       return (Number(b.scoreProb || 0) - Number(a.scoreProb || 0));
L0086: 
L0087:     });
L0088: 
L0089: }
L0090: 
L0091: export {
L0092: scoreItem,
L0093:   scoreRanking,
L0094:   calculateEvidenceStrength,
L0095: };

L0012: 
L0013: function normalizeEvidenceValue(value) {
L0014:   const n = Number(value);
L0015:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n <= 0) return 0;

L0017: }

L0019: function calculateEvidenceStrength(evidenceList = []) {
L0020:   const list = Array.isArray(evidenceList) ? evidenceList : [];
L0021: 
L0022:   if (!list.length) {
L0023:     return {
L0024:       score: 0,
L0025:       confidence: 0,
L0026:       reasons: ["Sem evidências suficientes."],
L0027:       signals: {},
L0028:     };
L0029:   }
L0030: 
L0031:   const values = list.map((e) => normalizeEvidenceValue(e.value));
L0032:   const total = values.reduce((acc, v) => acc + v, 0);
L0033:   const max = Math.max(...values, 1);
L0034: 
L0035:   const score = Math.min(100, Math.round((total / max) * 50));
L0036:   const confidence = Math.min(100, Math.round(score * Math.min(1, list.length / 3)));
L0037: 
L0038:   const reasons = list.flatMap((e) => Array.isArray(e.reasons) ? e.reasons : []);
L0039: 
L0040:   const signals = {};
L0041:   for (const e of list) {
L0042:     signals[e.module] = e.evidence || {};
L0043:   }
L0044: 
L0045:   return {
L0046:     score,
L0047:     confidence,
L0048:     reasons,
L0049:     signals,
L0050:   };
L0051: }
L0052: 
L0053: function scoreItem(item = {}, context = {}) {
L0054:   const collected = collectEvidence({
L0055:     item,
L0056:     context,
L0057:     config: scoreConfig,
L0058:   });
L0059: 
L0060:   const strength = calculateEvidenceStrength(collected.evidence);
L0061: 
L0062:   const probability = Math.max(
L0063:     0,
L0064:     Math.min(
L0065:       100,
L0066:       Number(item.scoreProb || item.rawScoreProb || 0) * 100
L0067:     )
L0068:   );
L0069: 
L0070:   const finalScore = Math.round(
L0071:     (strength.score * 0.45) +
L0072:     (probability * 0.55)
L0073:   );
L0074: 
L0075:   const finalConfidence = Math.round(
L0076:     (strength.confidence * 0.40) +
L0077:     (probability * 0.60)
L0078:   );
L0079: 
L0080:   return {
L0081:     ...item,
L0082: 
L0083:     score: finalScore,
L0084: 
L0085:     confidence: finalConfidence,
L0086: 
L0087:     reasons: [
L0088:       ...strength.reasons,
L0089:       `Probabilidade V3: ${probability.toFixed(2)}%`,
L0090:     ],
L0091: 
L0092:     signals: {
L0093:       ...strength.signals,
L0094:       probability,
L0095:     },
L0096: 
L0097:     evidenceCount: collected.count,
L0098: 
L0099:     evidenceModules: collected.modules,
L0100:   };
L0101: }
L0102: 
L0103: function scoreRanking(items = [], context = {}) {
L0104: 
L0105:   return (Array.isArray(items) ? items : [])
L0106:     .map(item => scoreItem(item, context))
L0107:     .sort((a, b) => {
L0108: 
L0109:       if (b.score !== a.score)
L0110:         return b.score - a.score;
L0111: 
L0112:       if (b.confidence !== a.confidence)
L0113:         return b.confidence - a.confidence;
L0114: 
L0115:       return (Number(b.scoreProb || 0) - Number(a.scoreProb || 0));
L0116: 
L0117:     });
L0118: 
L0119: }
L0120:

L0124:   calculateEvidenceStrength,
L0125: };

L0164: function rankTransitionMap(rowMap, basedOnGrupo, topN) {
L0165:   if (!rowMap || !rowMap.size) return [];
L0166: 
L0167:   const total = Array.from(rowMap.values()).reduce(
L0168:     (acc, n) => acc + safeInt(n, 0),
L0169:     0
L0170:   );
L0171: 
L0172:   if (total <= 0) return [];
L0173: 
L0174:   return Array.from(rowMap.entries())
L0175:     .sort((a, b) => {
L0176:       if (Number(b[1]) !== Number(a[1])) return Number(b[1]) - Number(a[1]);
L0177:       return Number(a[0]) - Number(b[0]);
L0178:     })
L0179:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, safeInt(topN, 3)))
L0180:     .map(([grupo, freq], i) => {
L0181:       const prob = Number(freq) / total;
L0182: 
L0183:       return {
L0184:         rank: i + 1,
L0185:         grupo: Number(grupo),
L0186:         freq: Number(freq),
L0187:         prob,
L0188:         probPct: Number((prob * 100).toFixed(2)),
L0189:         basedOnGrupo: Number(basedOnGrupo),
L0190:         totalTransitionsFromGrupo: total,
L0191:       };
L0192:     });
L0193: }

L0195: export function predictNextGrupoFromChain(arg1, arg2 = 3) {

L0197:     const draws = arg1;
L0198:     const topN = arg2;
L0199: 
L0200:     const list = dedupeAndSortDraws(draws);
L0201:     if (!list.length) return [];
L0202: 
L0203:     const { transitionsAny } = computeChainTransitions(list);
L0204:     const lastDraw = list[list.length - 1];
L0205:     const lastGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(lastDraw));
L0206: 
L0207:     if (!Number.isFinite(lastGrupo) || lastGrupo < 1 || lastGrupo > 25) {
L0208:       return [];
L0209:     }
L0210: 
L0211:     const rowMap = transitionsAny.get(lastGrupo);
L0212:     const ranked = rankTransitionMap(rowMap, lastGrupo, topN);
L0213: 
L0214:     return ranked.map((x) => ({
L0215:       ...x,
L0216:       mode: "legacy_any",
L0217:       basedOnHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(lastDraw)),
L0218:       basedOnYmd: pickDrawYMD(lastDraw),
L0219:       targetHour: "",
L0220:       targetYmd: "",

L0223:     }));
L0224:   }

L0195: function ymdHourToTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
L0196:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
L0197:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
L0198: 
L0199:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
L0200: 
L0201:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
L0202:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
L0203:     return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
L0204:   }
L0205: 
L0206:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
L0207:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
L0208: 
L0209:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
L0210: }

L0194: 
L0195: function ymdHourToTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
L0196:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
L0197:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
L0198: 
L0199:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
L0200: 
L0201:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
L0202:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
L0203:     return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
L0204:   }
L0205: 
L0206:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
L0207:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
L0208: 
L0209:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
L0210: }

L0195: function ymdHourToTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
L0196:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
L0197:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
L0198: 
L0199:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
L0200: 
L0201:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
L0202:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
L0203:     return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
L0204:   }
L0205: 
L0206:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
L0207:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
L0208: 
L0209:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
L0210: }

L3217: function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {

L3230: function buildSceneFromDraw(draw) {
L3231:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];

L3253:   const milhares = rows.map((x) => x.milhar).filter(Boolean);
L3254:

L3256:     const m = new Map();
L3257:     for (const x of arr) m.set(String(x), (m.get(String(x)) || 0) + 1);
L3258:     return Array.from(m.entries())
L3259:       .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
L3260:       .map(([value, count]) => ({ value, count }));
L3261:   };
L3262: 
L3263:   return {
L3264:     ymd: pickDrawYMD(draw),
L3265:     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw)),
L3266:     firstGrupo: grupos[0] ?? null,
L3267:     grupos,
L3268:     dezenas,
L3269:     centenas,

L3271:     repeatedGroups: countMap(grupos),
L3272:     repeatedDezenas: countMap(dezenas),
L3273:     repeatedCentenas: countMap(centenas),
L3274:     rows,
L3275:     signature: grupos.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join("-"),
L3276:   };
L3277: }

L0035: function normalizeMilharStr(v) {
L0036:   const s = String(v || "").trim();
L0037:   if (!s) return "";
L0038:   const dig = s.replace(/\D+/g, "");
L0039:   if (!dig) return "";
L0040:   return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
L0041: }

L0059:   if (!isYMD(s)) return s || "—";

L0061:   return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
L0062: }

L0064: function addDaysYMDLocal(ymd, amount) {
L0065:   const s = String(ymd || "").trim();
L0066:   if (!isYMD(s)) return "";
L0067: 
L0068:   const [y, m, d] = s.split("-").map(Number);
L0069:   const date = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
L0070: 
L0071:   date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() + Number(amount || 0));
L0072: 
L0073:   return [
L0074:     date.getUTCFullYear(),
L0075:     String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0"),
L0076:     String(date.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0"),
L0077:   ].join("-");
L0078: }

L0035: function normalizeMilharStr(v) {
L0036:   const s = String(v || "").trim();
L0037:   if (!s) return "";
L0038:   const dig = s.replace(/\D+/g, "");
L0039:   if (!dig) return "";
L0040:   return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
L0041: }
L0042:

L0051: }

L0053: function isYMD(s) {
L0054:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
L0055: }

L0057: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
L0058:   const s = String(ymd || "").trim();
L0059:   if (!isYMD(s)) return s || "—";
L0060:   const [y, m, d] = s.split("-");
L0061:   return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
L0062: }
